Method: 提出 BELXTR，将 XTR 的多向量（后期交互）架构扩展至生物医学实体链接：保留 token 级向量以进行细粒度匹配；集成已有的任务专用训练目标；并探索主动查询扩展等策略。

Result: 在十个语料库与五个知识库上的实验表明，BELXTR 在半数语料库上超越当前最优，平均 recall@1 提升 5 个百分点。最大收益体现在跨物种基因消歧子任务上：优于 LLM 驱动的检索-重排流水线，并接近专用规则系统。

Conclusion: 多向量模型可作为一种实用替代方案，用于替代难以维护的规则系统，或在 LLM 重排成本过高的 PubMed 规模挖掘场景中使用。代码已开源于 https://github.com/sg-wbi/belxtr。

Abstract: Biomedical Entity Linking disambiguates mentions to entities in a knowledge base (KB), making it the cornerstone of information extraction pipelines. While embedding-based models are a popular approach for the task, they suffer from a key limitation. They compress mentions (and entities) into a single vector, forcing the model to average away crucial fine-grained differences. We present BELXTR, a novel embedding model based on the multi-vector (a.k.a. late interaction) architecture, which allows to leverage token-level matching information. BELXTR extends the original XTR model to biomedical entity linking by integrating an existing task-specific training objective and exploring active query expansion. Experiments across ten corpora and five KBs show that BELXTR improves upon current state-of-the-art in half of the corpora with an average improvement of 5pp recall@1. The largest gains are reported on the challenging cross-species gene disambiguation subtask, where BELXTR outperforms an LLM-powered retrieve-and-rerank pipeline and closely approaches a specialized rule-based system. Our results highlight multi-vector models as a practical alternative to hard-to-maintain rule-based systems or in scenarios where LLM-based reranking is too costly as in PubMed-scale mining. The code to reproduce our experiments can be found at: https://github.com/sg-wbi/belxtr.

</details>


### [101] [Isolated Sign Language Recognition for Icelandic Sign Language: Experiments in a Low-resource Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25862)
*Finnur Ágúst Ingimundarson,Guðný Björk Þorvaldsdóttir,Mathias Müller,Sarah Ebling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对冰岛手语（ÍTM）进行孤立手语识别实验，基于低资源的ÍTM SignWiki数据集（1845个视频、849类，86%的类别仅两个样本），比较OpenHands与SPOTER框架，结果显示SPOTER与MediaPipe姿态更优，且跨语言迁移（用美式手语等预训练）带来最大提升，最高准确率从任务22类、117类到849类分别达72.7%、47.9%和22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰岛手语是真正的低资源手语，缺乏可用于孤立手语识别的数据与基准；现有ISLR研究多集中于资源丰富的手语。本文希望填补这一空白，并探究在极低资源（近乎每类单样本跨签名者）下，现有框架、姿态估计器以及跨语言迁移能否带来有效提升。

Method: 使用源自冰岛语—ÍTM双语在线词典的ÍTM SignWiki数据集（1845视频/849类）。比较两个开源ISLR框架OpenHands与SPOTER，在22、117、849类三个递增规模的识别任务上评估；测试三种姿态估计器（MediaPipe、AlphaPose、SDPose）；并进行两种跨语言迁移：其一用美式手语（ASL）数据预训练SPOTER后在ÍTM上微调，其二用六种其他手语的多语言数据训练OpenHands。

Result: 仅用ÍTM数据时，SPOTER在三个任务上均优于OpenHands，MediaPipe姿态优于AlphaPose和SDPose。跨语言迁移增益最大：ASL预训练后微调SPOTER使三个任务准确率提升14–24个百分点，分别达72.7%、47.9%、22.6%；多语言训练（六种手语）将OpenHands在全任务上从1.41%提升到28.86%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从资源更丰富的手语进行迁移对极低资源手语很有前景，尽管离实用仍有距离。作者公开了适配后的两个框架版本，以推动低资源手语识别研究。

Abstract: We present the first experiments on isolated sign language recognition (ISLR) for Icelandic Sign Language (ÍTM). We use ÍTM SignWiki, a dataset derived from a bilingual Icelandic--ÍTM online dictionary. It is genuinely low-resource: 1,845 videos cover 849 classes, 86% of which have only two examples, making the full task effectively one-shot recognition across signers. We compare two open-source ISLR frameworks, OpenHands and SPOTER, on three tasks of increasing vocabulary size (22, 117 and 849 classes), and evaluate three pose estimators and two forms of cross-lingual transfer. With ÍTM data alone, SPOTER outperforms OpenHands on all three tasks, and MediaPipe poses give better results than AlphaPose or SDPose. Cross-lingual transfer brings the largest gains: pretraining SPOTER on American Sign Language data before finetuning on ÍTM raises accuracy by 14--24 percentage points, to 72.7%, 47.9% and 22.6% on the three tasks, and multilingual training with data from six other sign languages lifts OpenHands from 1.41% to 28.86% on the full task. Although far from practical use, the results suggest that transfer from better-resourced sign languages is promising for very low-resource ones. We release our adapted versions of both frameworks.

</details>


### [102] [Rethinking Length-Based Training: Batch Composition and Loss Normalization in Speech Token Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25890)
*Hongjin Song,Runwu Shi,Weiqiao Shan,Jiale Luo,Yujin Wang,Yifei Wu,Chunxiang Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统拆解了语音模型“短到长”课程训练中多个混淆因素，发现当固定批次组成与token暴露量时，短到长排序本身并无独立收益；所观察到的增益主要来源于token加权与first-epoch分组等机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “短到长”课程训练是一种简单的语音模型训练策略，但其带来的增益难以归因，因为长度训练会同时改变扰动策略、批次组成、token保留以及batch-mean损失下的token权重等多个因素。作者希望解耦这些混杂因素，明确究竟哪一个因素真正带来收益。

Method: 通过匹配对照实验，逐一固定批次组成与token暴露等条件，解耦长度训练的各影响因素；分别在batch-mean损失和token-balanced损失下评估first-epoch分组的效果，并在不同tokenizer之间进行交叉验证，以考察chunk长度变化与token加权之间的联系。

Result: 在测试设定下，当批次组成和token暴露被固定时，短到长排序没有独立的收益；first-epoch分组在batch-mean损失下降低了Mimi的困惑度，但这一增益在token-balanced损失下并未出现；跨tokenizer的结果与“chunk长度变化与token加权之间存在关联”的假设一致。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一套系统分析协议，用于研究变长语音模型中的基于长度的训练策略，并指出短到长课程本身的增益可能源于token加权等混淆因素而非排序本身。

Abstract: Short-to-long training is a simple curriculum for speech models, but its gains can be difficult to interpret. In speech token language models, length-based training can change the shuffle policy, batch composition, token retention, and token weights under batch-mean loss. We disentangle these factors through matched comparisons. In the tested settings, short-to-long ordering shows no independent benefit when batch composition and token exposure are fixed. First-epoch grouping lowers perplexity for Mimi under batch-mean loss, but this gain is not observed under token-balanced loss. The cross-tokenizer results are consistent with a link between chunk-length variation and token weighting. This work provides a systematic analysis protocol for studying length-based training in variable-length speech models.

</details>


### [103] [Informed Masking: Structure-Aware Perturbation for Reinforcement Learning in Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25927)
*Xiaoyi Yu,Enver Sangineto,Pei Fu,Fiorenzo Parascandolo,Wenhui Tan,Ruikang Zhang,Rita Cucchiara,Ruihua Song,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散大语言模型(dLLM)的强化学习对齐问题,发现不同token的掩码重建子问题存在难度不对称性,提出无需额外推理开销的Informed Masking(IM)掩码采样策略,将掩码偏向"下游token",在LLaDA-8B-Instruct上使三种SOTA RL方法在数学与规划基准上获得最高2.01%、8.68%、5.77%的相对平均增益,并提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM的RL对齐需要从掩码重建子问题中以小蒙特卡洛预算估计似然代理(likelihood surrogates),但现有方法采用均匀随机掩码构造子问题,未考虑优先处理哪些子问题才能更有效地服务于似然估计与策略优化。

Method: 作者识别出dLLM rollout中系统性的上游/下游结构:被揭示时会引发邻近未解码位置置信度大幅变化的token称为"上游",仅引起局部小变化的token称为"下游"。发现掩码下游token产生的子问题比掩码上游token更好求解,称为"子问题难度不对称性"。据此提出Informed Masking(IM):从去噪轨迹中以零额外推理开销计算每个token的优先级分数,使掩码采样偏向下游token,且即插即用。

Result: 将IM接入三种SOTA dLLM RL方法(应用于LLaDA-8B-Instruct),在数学与规划基准上获得最高2.01%、8.68%、5.77%的相对平均增益,同时训练稳定性得到改善。

Conclusion: 通过揭示dLLM rollout中的上游/下游token结构及其导致的子问题难度不对称性,提出的Informed Masking以零额外开销的优先级掩码采样显著提升了dLLM RL对齐的性能与稳定性,验证了非均匀、信息导向的子问题构造对RL代理估计的重要性。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as an efficient alternative to autoregressive models, yet aligning them via Reinforcement Learning (RL) requires likelihood surrogates estimated from masked reconstruction subproblems under a small Monte Carlo budget per rollout. Existing methods construct these subproblems by uniform random masking, leaving open the question of which subproblems to prioritize. We identify a systematic upstream/downstream structure in dLLM rollouts. Some tokens, when revealed, trigger large confidence changes in nearby undecoded positions; we call them upstream. Others induce only small local changes and are therefore downstream. We find masking downstream tokens yields substantially better-posed subproblems than masking upstream tokens, a phenomenon we term subproblem difficulty asymmetry. Based on the observation, we propose Informed Masking (IM), which derives a per-token priority score from the denoising trajectory at zero extra inference cost and biases mask sampling toward downstream tokens. IM is plug-and-play: when plugged into three state-of-the-art dLLM RL methods on LLaDA-8B-Instruct, it delivers up to 2.01%, 8.68%, and 5.77% relative average gains on math and planning benchmarks with improved training stability.

</details>


### [104] [ClusterFewshot: Improving Few-shot Optimization for LLMs workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25939)
*Omri Bar Haim,Shahar Katz,Lior Wolf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClusterFewshot 提出一种结合语义聚类结构与效用感知评分的少样本示例选择策略，在 DSPy 流程中显著降低优化成本并提升多个基准上相对于 bootstrap 方法的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 工作流的性能往往取决于选取少量上下文示例来引导模型行为。现有方法通过融入成功的推理路径来增强提示，但在演示选择上依赖随机采样或基于指标的排序，忽略了任务的语义结构，导致示例集缺乏代表性和有效性。

Method: 提出 ClusterFewshot，将语义结构化（对候选示例进行聚类以捕捉任务的语义分布）与效用感知评分（为示例分配效用分数并据此选择）相结合，从而构造具有代表性和有效性的少样本演示集。

Result: 在多个基准上，ClusterFewshot 大幅降低 DSPy 流水线的优化成本，同时无论单独提示调优还是混合提示-权重优化场景，都相对先前的 bootstrap 方法持续提升准确率。

Conclusion: 通过同时利用任务的语义结构与示例的效用信息，ClusterFewshot 在保证效果提升的同时显著削减了优化开销，展示了更结构化的演示选择方式优于随机采样和纯度量排序。

Abstract: The performance of large language model (LLM) workflows often depends on selecting a small set of in-context demonstrations to guide model behavior on new tasks. Recent methods improve this process by augmenting prompts with successful reasoning paths. However, their demonstration selection relies on random sampling or metric-based rankings, overlooking the semantic structure of the task. We propose ClusterFewshot, a strategy that combines semantic structuring with utility-aware scoring to construct representative and effective few-shot demonstration sets. Evaluated within DSPy-based pipelines, ClusterFewshot substantially reduces optimization cost across multiple benchmarks, while consistently improving accuracy relative to prior bootstrap-based methods in both standalone prompt tuning and hybrid prompt-weight optimization.

</details>


### [105] [Domain-Adaptive Pretraining Enhances Water Treatment Semantic Representation for Large-Scale Structured Literature Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26034)
*Mudi Zhai,Ruihong Qiu,Qingyun Zeng,T. David Waite,Bing-Jie Ni,Haoran Duan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文开发了面向水处理领域的编码器模型 WaterBERT，通过大规模无监督预训练和下游微调实现高效语义表示与结构化信息抽取，并在此基础上构建了知识图谱与知识增强检索系统 WaterKERS，显著提升了水处理文献挖掘的性能与成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水处理研究文献增长迅速，但领域知识分散在非结构化文本中，现有通用语言模型无法充分捕获水处理领域的特定语义，缺乏专门的语言模型支撑大规模文献挖掘与证据整合。

Method: 在约29.7亿 token 的水处理语料上对 BERT 进行持续预训练得到 WaterBERT；针对三个下游任务（处理工艺分类、命名实体识别、关系抽取）微调并系统评估；将 WaterBERT 与 BERTopic 结合做无监督主题识别；利用 WaterBERT 处理69万余条摘要构建结构化知识图谱，并与词法检索和稠密检索融合，开发 WaterKERS 检索系统。

Result: WaterBERT 在下游任务中全面优于通用及领域专用 BERT：处理工艺分类 F1 90.12%，命名实体识别 F1 79.50%，关系抽取 F1 74.04%；在5,144篇 ES&T 文章中识别出连贯、多样且领域专属的主题；以远低于商用 LLM 的成本处理693,211条摘要并保持有竞争力的抽取性能；WaterKERS 相关性得分77.7，显著高于纯文本检索基线（54.7-64.5）。

Conclusion: WaterBERT 为水处理研究提供了一种紧凑、可扩展的语义基础，能够支撑大规模信息处理与证据图谱构建，有望加速该领域的知识整合与科学发现。

Abstract: Water treatment research is expanding rapidly, but much of the knowledge acquired from this research remains scattered across unstructured literature. The field still lacks a dedicated language model that can efficiently capture water treatment-specific domain semantics for large-scale literature mining. Here, we address this by developing WaterBERT, a domain-adapted encoder model designed for semantic representation and structured information extraction from water treatment texts. WaterBERT was developed by continual pretraining on a large-scale water treatment corpus comprising about 2.97 billion tokens. Three fine-tuned models based on WaterBERT were systematically evaluated on downstream tasks, achieving the best overall performance among general-purpose and domain-specific BERT models, with F1 scores of 90.12% for multiclass treatment process classification, 79.50% for named entity recognition, and 74.04% for relation extraction. Beyond these benchmark tasks, we further demonstrated WaterBERT's advantages for large-scale literature processing. Applied to 5,144 Environmental Science & Technology articles, WaterBERT-BERTopic identified coherent, diverse, and domain-specific research topics without predefined categories. Building on WaterBERT, we processed 693,211 abstracts at substantially lower cost than commercial LLMs while retaining competitive extraction performance to construct a structured water treatment knowledge graph. The knowledge graph was then integrated with lexical and dense retrieval to develop a Water Knowledge-Enhanced Retrieval System (WaterKERS), which achieved a relevance score of 77.7, substantially outperforming text-based retrieval baselines (54.7-64.5). Through WaterBERT, this study provides a compact and scalable semantic foundation for large-scale information processing and evidence mapping in water treatment research.

</details>


### [106] [Truth for Believable AI: Expressed Doubt, Provenance, and Belief Revision as an Engineerable Stance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26035)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究能否在固定语言模型之上叠加一个"认知行为层"，用于表达不确定性、来源感知断言和显式信念修正。通过合成模型与Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct的多会话基准测试发现：审计性确认具有构造性保证（100%成立），真实纠正比虚假纠正更易被接受，但基于token概率的不确定性门控端到端正确率低于随机（AUC 0.41），而基于采样一致性的门控可区分（AUC 0.66），一致性门控配置在独立协议下复现了会话级操控与能力等价指标，但结果依赖选择且尚未在60个事实层面解决相关准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话代理通常以统一自信的口吻表达答案，缺乏对不确定性、信息来源和信念修正的表达能力。作者希望检验这类能力是否可作为"行为层"叠加在固定语言模型之上实现，而非改变模型本身，从而在不评估可信度或可相信性的前提下，提升代理表达的可审计性和诚实性。

Method: 在固定语言模型上叠加一个行为层，组合三种认知状态：逐声明置信度与类型化来源标注、来源门控表达规则、带可审计确认的持久修正存储（对虚假纠正具有部分抵抗力）。在构造的、可机械评分的多会话基准上，使用合成模型与Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct进行评估。包含五个预注册校验、一个预设协议，以及一个披露式事后分析（比较平均答案token概率门控与采样一致性门控的判别能力，以AUC衡量），并在重新抽取的会话集上复现一致性门控配置。

Result: 合成仪器通过全部五个校验。在真实模型上，确认健全性（构造性保证）100%成立；真实纠正比虚假纠正更易被接受（在已持有信念上0.44 vs 0.15；若计入规则接受的对未持有事实的纠正则为0.875 vs 0.420）。但预设的表达保真度、矛盾分离与来源边距指标均未达标。事后分析显示：以平均答案token概率门控表达时，端到端正确率排序低于随机（AUC 0.41，会话聚类）；以采样一致性门控则具有判别力（AUC 0.66）。一致性门控配置在独立承诺协议下满足会话级操控与能力等价准则，并在重抽会话集上复现；但操控结果依赖于该选择，且当不确定性在60个事实上聚类时两项准则仍未解决。

Conclusion: 得到支持结论仅限于：构造性审计保证、部分依赖于修正存储的纠错区分能力，以及token概率门控在特定基准与模型上的失败。需要扩展事实库规模后再进行人类评估。结果提示需谨慎对待不确定性表达的门控机制选择，且当前发现尚不足以支撑广泛推广。

Abstract: Conversational agents often express answers in a uniformly confident register. We test whether expressed uncertainty, provenance-aware assertion, and explicit belief revision can be implemented as a behavior layer over a fixed language model; we do not test believability or trust. The layer combines three epistemic states, per-claim confidence and typed provenance, a provenance-gated expression rule, and a persistent revision store with auditable acknowledgments and partial resistance to false corrections. We evaluate it on a constructed, mechanically scored multi-session benchmark using a synthetic model and Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct. The synthetic instrument passes all five checks. On the real model, acknowledgment soundness, a by-construction guarantee, holds in 100% of cases, and true corrections are accepted more often than false ones (0.44 vs. 0.15 on held beliefs; 0.875 vs. 0.420 including rule-accepted corrections of unheld facts), but the pre-specified expression-fidelity, contradiction-separation, and provenance margins fail. A disclosed post hoc analysis shows that expression gated on mean answer-token probability ranks correctness below chance end to end (AUC 0.41, conversation-clustered), whereas gating on sampling consistency discriminates (AUC 0.66). A consistency-gated configuration selected from this finding and evaluated under a separately committed protocol meets the conversation-level manipulation and capability-equivalence criteria and replicates on a redrawn conversation set. The manipulation result is selection-dependent, and both criteria remain unresolved when uncertainty is clustered over the 60 facts. The supported conclusions are limited to the by-construction audit guarantee, store-dependent partial correction discrimination, and a benchmark- and model-specific failure of token-probability gating; scaling the fact base is required before human evaluation.

</details>


### [107] [Optimizing Denoising Trajectories in dLLMs: A Lightweight Evolutionary Heuristic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26052)
*Zijian Zhao,Dian Jin,Xialiang Tong,Sen Li,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）推理时的去噪调度器问题，分析了现有置信度启发式调度器的两种失败模式（EOS溢出与近端偏置），揭示其根源于注意力机制对无效token分配过高权重产生误导性置信信号，并提出一种基于CMA-ES优化的轻量进化启发式调度器，以393个可训练参数动态融合多种启发式特征与上下文平均场嵌入，在多个推理与规划基准上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLMs通过双向注意力和并行解码实现高效生成，但推理时需要精心设计的去噪调度器（训练时不存在），其选择显著影响生成质量。现有基于置信度的启发式调度器虽然经验表现良好，但存在EOS溢出和近端偏置两种关键失败模式，且最优去噪轨迹高度依赖上下文，单一指标无法在所有场景下一致表现优异，因此需要一个能动态适应上下文的高效调度方法。

Method: 作者首先深入分析Transformer的注意力模式，发现失败源于某些位置对无效token（如[MASK]和[EOS]）分配过高注意力权重，产生误导性置信信号；继而证明有效注意力分数可作为置信度启发式的互补指导。在此基础上，提出一种轻量进化启发式调度器，使用协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）进行优化，动态融合多种启发式特征与上下文平均场嵌入，仅需393个可训练参数。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream两个模型上，于四个推理与规划基准上评估，该方法一致地优于强基线，包括传统启发式方法、块自回归方法以及最新的SOTA方法；同时该方法成为迄今已知参数效率最高的神经调度器（仅393个参数）。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了dLLMs现有置信度调度器失败的根本原因（注意力对无效token的过度关注导致误导性置信信号），并提出用有效注意力分数补充置信度信号；所提出的CMA-ES优化的轻量进化启发式调度器以极少参数实现上下文自适应，在多个基准上取得最优性能，为dLLMs的高效、高质量推理去噪调度提供了参数高效的新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to conventional Auto-Regressive (AR) Large Language Models (LLMs). By leveraging bidirectional attention and parallel decoding, dLLMs enable more efficient generation. However, they require a carefully designed denoising scheduler at inference time (absent during training) whose choice significantly impacts generation quality. While confidence-based heuristic schedulers have shown strong empirical performance, they suffer from two critical failure modes: EOS Overflow and Proximal Bias. Through in-depth analysis of the Transformer's attention patterns, we reveal that these failures stem from certain positions assigning disproportionately high attention weights to invalid tokens (e.g., [MASK] and [EOS]), which produce misleading confidence signals. Building on this insight, empirical evidence shows that valid attention scores can provide complementary guidance to conventional confidence-based heuristics, yet no single metric consistently excels across all scenarios, implying that the optimal denoising trajectory is highly context-dependent. To address this problem, we propose a lightweight evolutionary heuristic scheduler optimized using the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES). Our scheduler dynamically integrates multiple heuristic features with a contextual mean-field embedding, while requiring only 393 trainable parameters. Evaluated on LLaDA and Dream across four reasoning and planning benchmarks, our method consistently outperforms strong baselines, including conventional heuristics, block auto-regressive methods, and recent State-Of-The-Art (SOTA) approaches. To the best of our knowledge, it represents the most parameter-efficient neural scheduler to date. Our code is available at https://github.com/RS2002/Evo-Denoise .

</details>


### [108] [CoVeR: Coverage-Based Routing of Verifier Calls in Agentic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26086)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 CoVeR 门控机制：用一个冻结的句子嵌入覆盖裕度阈值，在检索过程中跳过大部分 LLM 验证调用，仅对模糊状态调用验证器，从而在几乎不损失答案准确率的前提下大幅削减验证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式检索系统每步需判断证据是否足够以决定停止，交给 LLM 验证器虽可靠但需反复处理不断增长的证据，带来大量重复计算开销。作者希望证明这些验证调用大多可被跳过而不明显降低答案准确率。

Method: 提出 CoVeR（Coverage-based Verifier Routing）：在冻结的句子嵌入上计算覆盖裕度，用单一阈值检测证据明显不完整的状态直接跳过验证，仅在模糊的剩余状态调用验证器。该门控可蒸馏为一个 921k 参数的小头，脱离 LLM。

Result: 在三个多跳 QA 基准上（评估协议预先固定），CoVeR 门控代理与全预算代理及始终验证基线在 EM 分数上相差不到一个点；减少 62-68% 的验证调用，饱和场景下减少 93%。基于证据计数、词法重叠、BM25 相关性的路由器（单独或联合学习）总体权衡更差；门控可跨决策器和代理规模零调优迁移。

Conclusion: 同一信号不能替代验证——匹配一个声明远比判断该声明是否得到支持容易。CoVeR 作为覆盖门控能以极低成本筛选出需验证的模糊状态，实现近乎无损的验证调用削减。

Abstract: An agentic retrieval system issues a sequence of search queries and must decide, at each step, whether the evidence collected so far is enough to stop. Delegating that decision to an LLM verifier or a prompt judge makes stopping reliable, but the verifier then reprocesses the growing evidence after every retrieval step, a substantial repeated cost. We show that most of these calls can be skipped without materially changing answer accuracy: a single threshold on a frozen sentence-embedding coverage margin detects the states in which the evidence is still plainly incomplete, and the verifier is called only on the ambiguous remainder, a gate we call CoVeR (Coverage-based Verifier Routing). Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks, with the evaluation protocol fixed before the full-scale run, the CoVeR-gated agent matches the answer accuracy of both the full-budget agent and the always-verify baseline within a fraction of an EM point. It cuts 62-68% of verifier calls, and 93% in a saturated regime. Routers built on evidence counts, lexical overlap, or BM25 relevance, alone or learned in combination, give weaker overall trade-offs, the gate transfers without re-tuning across deciders and agent scales, and its drafter distills into a 921k-parameter head atop the frozen encoder, leaving no LLM in the routing loop. The same signal cannot replace verification: matching a claim is far easier than deciding the claim is supported.

</details>


### [109] [SpecialEduBench: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Knowledge, Skill, and Attitude in Language Intervention for Autistic Children](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26090)
*Jihoi Na,Taeyeong Kim,Sungjune Kong,Jaemin Jung,Min Joung Park,Kyungtae Joo,Ahhyun Kim,Shim Jaechang,Sooyoung Joo,Dongjin Ka,SeJoong Kim,Jimin Kim,HyunJin Jung,Unggi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向自闭症儿童早期干预的评测基准 SpecialEduBench，从知识、技能、态度三个维度衡量视觉语言模型的教学能力，并发现当前前沿模型在该领域仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自闭症儿童早期干预大多以语言为目标，但目标和方法因儿童而异，需要教师一对一、根据实时场景即时判断。现有进入特殊教育领域的 AI 基准只考察"模型知道什么"，而非"模型在孩子面前做了什么"；且教学是否得当取决于儿童刚刚的反应，没有标准答案，判断依据既是言语性也是视觉性的（等待时长、目光转移、儿童反应等），这些都是转录文本无法保留的。因此需要构建一个能真正衡量教学胜任力的基准。

Method: 作者构建了 SpecialEduBench，从知识（knowledge）、技能（skill）、态度（attitude）三个维度度量教学胜任力：包含 4,537 道知识题、200 道技能题和 68 道态度题（后者基于真实干预录音，并将"压力"与"监控"两个因素交叉形成 192 个反应单元）。七名特殊教育专家负责编写、评分与审校题目，并依据专家给出的参考分数反复修订评判模型的指令。随后在八种前沿视觉语言模型上进行评测。

Result: 八个前沿视觉语言模型在 SpecialEduBench 上没有任何一个维度达到饱和：最强模型在态度维度的"诚实性"单元上仍失误约十分之一。各模型在事实性知识上表现趋同，在情境化（situated）任务上表现分化，且失误集中在施加压力（pressure）的场景中。

Conclusion: SpecialEduBench 可作为特殊教育 AI 部署前的审计工具，并为面向该领域构建专门模型提供起点。当前通用前沿模型尚不足以胜任自闭症儿童早期干预这一情境化、高要求的教学任务。

Abstract: Language is the target of most early intervention for autistic children. Because the goal and the method change from child to child, the work falls to a teacher who takes one child at a time and judges each scene as it unfolds. Artificial intelligence is now being brought to that work, yet the benchmarks that reach special education ask what a model knows rather than what it does in front of a child. Building one is not straightforward, since whether a response is good teaching depends on what the child has just done, so no answer key applies. The evidence that settles it is visual as much as verbal, since the length of a wait, a shift of gaze, and the child's uptake leave no trace in a transcript. We introduce \emph{SpecialEduBench}, which measures pedagogical competence along knowledge, skill, and attitude, with 4,537 knowledge items and with 200 skill items and 68 attitude items built on recorded intervention, the attitude items crossing pressure with monitoring into 192 response cells. Seven special-education experts wrote, scored, and reviewed the items, and we revised the judge model's instruction against the reference scores they set. Across eight frontier vision-language models no axis is saturated, since the strongest still fails about a tenth of the honesty cells. The models converge where the knowledge is factual and separate where the task is situated, and the failures gather where pressure is applied. We intend the benchmark as an audit to run before deployment and as a starting point for models built for this domain.

</details>


### [110] [One Domain, Many Tongues: Composing Domain and Language LoRAs for Cross-Lingual Remote-Sensing MLLMs without Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26097)
*Xuechen Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MODL（互正交域-语言组合）方法，通过将领域 LoRA 与语言 LoRA 在每一层保持互正交的联合训练，在不使用任何多语言遥感样本的情况下，为英文遥感多模态大模型添加新语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感多模态大模型仅在英文上训练和评估，而纯文本指令数据覆盖 100 多种语言；现有方法（如训练-free 融合或现有正交变体）在添加语言时要么回答正确但仍是英文，要么破坏基座模型的多语言文本能力，甚至出现发散问题。

Method: MODL 核心是在单一项损失约束下联合训练两个 LoRA：一个基于英文遥感图像训练的领域 LoRA，一个仅基于纯文本训练的语言 LoRA；约束使两者在每一层的更新始终保持互正交。

Result: MODL 在每个随机种子上都修复了所有失败：答案正确且用目标语言回答的比例为 56-71%，最优的替代方法仅 27%，多数低于 8%；文本能力保持与未训练基座相同水平；在西班牙语上超越 Qwen2.5-VL-7B，且全程零多语言-多模态数据。单一五语言适配器在三个种子上完整保留英语、西班牙语和越南语能力；非拉丁文字仍是待突破边界。

Conclusion: 互正交约束是 MODL 发挥作用的关键成分，该方法能够在不引入任何多语言遥感数据的前提下，为新语言添加遥感问答能力并保持原有文本能力，但非拉丁文字脚本仍是当前方法的局限。

Abstract: Remote-sensing (RS) multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are trained and evaluated only in English, while text-only instruction data covers over 100 languages. We propose MODL (Mutually Orthogonal Domain-Language composition), a recipe that adds new languages to an English RS MLLM without a single multilingual RS example: a domain LoRA trained on English RS imagery and a language LoRA trained on text alone are learned jointly, under one loss term that keeps the two updates mutually orthogonal at every layer throughout training. This constraint is the recipe's active ingredient. Without it, the same training answers RS questions correctly but in English, erases much of the base model's multilingual text ability, and diverges on one seed in three; sixteen alternatives, from training-free merging to prior orthogonality variants, fail the same way. MODL repairs every failure on every seed: answers are correct and in the target language 56-71% of the time, where the best alternative reaches 27% and most stay below 8%, text ability stays at the level of the untrained base, and on Spanish it surpasses Qwen2.5-VL-7B, with zero multilingual-multimodal data. A single five-language adapter retains English, Spanish, and Vietnamese at full strength across three seeds; non-Latin scripts remain an open boundary.

</details>


### [111] [TSS: Target-Side Sparsification for Speculative Decoding in Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26100)
*Haibo Hu,Lianming Huang,Qiao Li,Nan Guan,Chun Jason Xue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TSS（Target-Side Sparsification）框架，在推测解码中跳过目标模型的部分层以减少验证成本，并同时提升草稿接受率和下游任务性能，实现无需重训练或永久剪枝的多稀疏验证路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法主要优化草稿模型一侧，目标验证器通常保持稠密不变。作者发现，在特定领域推理下，全深度目标验证并非总是最优：跳过选定的目标层既能降低验证成本，又能提高草稿接受率，甚至不损失或提升下游任务性能。这一反直觉现象促使作者探究目标侧的稀疏化。

Method: 提出 TSS 目标侧稀疏化框架：采用兼顾接受率与指标的广度搜索（acceptance- and metric-aware breadth search），在不强制两个目标固定优先级的前提下探索多层跳过的配置；将选出的配置存储为“领域到配置”的映射，并由轻量级跳过控制器（skip controller）在推理时应用。该设计使同一个完整目标模型可支持多条稀疏验证路径，无需重新训练或永久性参数剪枝。

Result: 在 Spec-Bench 上跨多个领域、多种模型规模和多种推测解码方法均获得一致的草稿接受率与下游任务性能提升。在翻译任务中，平均接受长度从 2.70 提升到 4.53（+67.8%），BLEU 从 0.131 提升到 0.237（+80.9%），端到端吞吐量从 75.6 tokens/s 提升到 127.3 tokens/s，相当于 1.68 倍加速。

Conclusion: 全深度目标验证并非推测解码的最优选择，通过目标侧层跳过可在降低验证开销的同时提升接受率与任务性能。TSS 通过自动搜索和轻量控制提供了无需重训练、可灵活切换的稀疏验证方案，在 Spec-Bench 上验证了其跨领域、跨规模和跨方法的有效性与加速效果。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference through collaboration between a lightweight draft model and a target verifier. Existing methods mainly improve the draft side, while the target model is typically kept dense and unchanged. We show that, under domain-specific inference, full-depth target verification is not always the optimal choice. Counter-intuitively, skipping selected target layers can reduce verification cost while simultaneously increasing draft acceptance and preserving, or even improving, downstream task performance. Based on this observation, we propose TSS, a target-side sparsification framework for speculative decoding. TSS employs an acceptance- and metric-aware breadth search to explore multi-layer skip configurations without imposing a fixed priority between the two objectives. The selected configurations are stored in a domain-to-configuration mapping and applied by a lightweight skip controller, allowing one complete target model to support multiple sparse verification paths without retraining or permanent parameter pruning. Experiments on Spec-Bench across multiple domains, model scales, and speculative decoding methods show consistent improvements in draft acceptance and downstream task performance. In Translation setting, TSS increases the average accept length from 2.70 to 4.53 (+67.8%), improves BLEU from 0.131 to 0.237 (+80.9%), and raises end-to-end throughput from 75.6 to 127.3 tokens/s, corresponding to a 1.68X speedup.

</details>


### [112] [Differentiable Fuzzy Inference Layer: A Monotone, Compositional Ordinal Reasoning Head for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26113)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出可微模糊推理层（DFIL），通过在标准分类头旁并联一个基于有序隶属函数库、带标量瓶颈的分支，赋予语言模型对序数类别进行单调性与组合推理的能力，从而修复“most of most”这类复合量词被错误归类为“most”的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在解释“most of most students passed”时错误地回答“most”，而两个“most”复合后比例应更接近“some”。作者将这一失败归因于架构设计缺陷：标准分类头将序数类别视为相互独立的标签，缺乏尊重其自然顺序并进行代数组合的机制，而非训练数据不足。

Method: 提出DFIL（可微模糊推理层），这是一个双路径预测头：将标准分类器与一个受有序隶属函数库约束、带标量瓶颈的分支配对。该标量分支提供两个结构原语——对底层量的单调性，以及无需组合训练数据、通过t-范数运算实现的组合推理；标量分支还提供了可解释的接口用于分析残差。

Result: DFIL在多个LLM家族的自然语言序数任务上完成了实例化与验证，能够修复“most of most”复合量词的组合推理错误，并通过标量分支实现对残差的可解释分析。

Conclusion: 通过引入尊重序数结构、支持组合推理的DFIL预测头，可以弥补仅依赖标签的分类头在序数语义与代数组合上的结构性缺陷，为语言模型的序数推理提供可解释且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: A state-of-the-art language model asked to interpret "most of most students passed" typically answers "most," though composing two instances of "most" yields a proportion closer to "some." We trace this failure to an architectural choice rather than a data deficit: standard classifier heads treat ordinal categories as independent labels, with no mechanism to respect their natural ordering or compose them algebraically. We introduce the Differentiable Fuzzy Inference Layer (DFIL), a dual-path prediction head pairing a standard classifier with a scalar-bottlenecked branch grounded in a bank of ordered membership functions. DFIL supplies two structural primitives that a label-only head cannot inherit: monotonicity in the underlying quantity, and compositional reasoning via t-norm operations without any compositional training data. The scalar branch additionally provides an interpretable interface for analyzing residual errors. We instantiate DFIL on ordinal natural-language tasks across diverse LLM families.

</details>


### [113] [Magnitude Profile Pruning: Calibration-Free Structured Attention Head Removal for Transformer Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26177)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Heranga K. Rathnasekara,Suranadi De Silva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练、无需校准数据的注意力头剪枝准则 MP（Magnitude Profile），通过对权重行范数做统计离群点检测来识别可移除的头，并给出适配 GQA 的变体 MP-G，在多个模型上以零前向、零梯度、零校准成本达到或超过依赖数据的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力头重要性度量方法通常需要校准数据、梯度计算或 Hessian 估计，带来额外开销并依赖数据分布。作者希望提出一种纯权重、零成本的裁剪准则，消除对数据和梯度的依赖。

Method: 提出 Magnitude Profile (MP) 评分：对每头投影权重的行范数做统计离群点检测，范数落在总体主体区间的头视为可裁减，而范数呈离群（承载不成比例表示能力）的头被保留。进一步提出 MP-G 变体，通过将共享的 key-value 组得分分配到关联的 query 头来处理 Grouped Query Attention (GQA)。

Result: 在五模型、WikiText-2 困惑度、12.5%–50% 头稀疏度下：MP-G 在 OPT-6.7B 所有稀疏度上困惑度最优（12.5% 为 18.46，25% 为 27.87，50% 为 152.0）；在 RoBERTa-large 的 12.5% 和 25% 稀疏度下也最优（7.27 与 10.28），超越 Wanda-Head、SparseGPT-Head、Gradient-Head 等依赖校准的基线。50% 稀疏度下头剪枝带来最高 16% 参数量减少和 50% 注意力 FLOPs 节省。

Conclusion: 仅基于权重的统计评分即可匹敌甚至超越依赖数据的方法，为 Transformer 结构化头剪枝提供了一种实用、零成本的准则。

Abstract: Structured pruning of attention heads provides a hardware-friendly way to compress Transformer language models. However, existing methods for measuring head-level importance require calibration data, gradient computation, or Hessian estimation. These requirements add extra overhead and make the methods depend on the data. Our work presents Magnitude Profile (MP) scoring, a training-free criterion for head importance that identifies dispensable heads through statistical outlier detection on weight row norms. Heads whose projection weights fall within the population bulk are pruned, while heads exhibiting outlier norms, which carry disproportionate representational capacity, are preserved. Our work further gives MP-G, a variant that handles Grouped Query Attention (GQA) by distributing shared key-value group scores across associated query heads. Across five models evaluated on WikiText-2 perplexity at 12.5%-50% head sparsity, MP-G achieves the best perplexity on OPT-6.7B at all sparsity levels (18.46 at 12.5%, 27.87 at 25%, 152.0 at 50%). MP-G also gives the best results on RoBERTa-large at 12.5% and 25% sparsity, with perplexity values of 7.27 and 10.28, outperforming calibration-dependent baselines including Wanda-Head, SparseGPT-Head, and Gradient-Head. It requires zero forward passes, calibration samples, or gradient computation. At 50% sparsity, head pruning yields up to 16% parameter reduction with 50% attention FLOP savings. Our results show that weight-only statistical scoring can match or outperform data-dependent methods for structured head pruning, providing a practical, zero-cost criterion for Transformer compression.

</details>


### [114] [Modality-Gated Deep Adapters: Adding a Modality to a Frozen Embedding Model with Exact Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26182)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy,Kazi Fardinul Hoque,Md. Shahrier Islam Arham,Arman Luthra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“模态门控深度适配器”（modality-gated deep adapters），在冻结的多模态嵌入LLM的每一解码层附加瓶颈适配器，并按模态打包，仅在编码本模态时执行，从而在不改变既有模型任何输出（逐位不变）的前提下新增模态能力，并在音频与热成像任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA风格适配）在扩展多模态嵌入模型到新模态时，会静默改变文本路径的输出，即使权重未合并也会破坏已存储的全部嵌入向量；大规模部署场景下（检索索引、基准结果、行为审计）无法容忍这种对基础模型精确输出的改写。因此需要一个能“零改动”新增模态的方案。

Method: 提出modality-gated deep adapters：将一个冻结的多模态嵌入LLM的每一解码层都挂接瓶颈适配器，并按模态分组为per-modality packs；每个pack只在其对应模态被编码时执行。未被任何pack认领的输入直接走基础模型自身计算图，输出逐位不变；共载的多个pack之间具有精确为零的隔离矩阵。该性质以命题形式陈述，在任意训练后（而非仅初始化时）依然成立，推理时无需任务标签或路由元数据。方法通过已发布检查点的精确相等性测试验证；音频pack以连接符token形式注入，热成像pack复用基础模型冻结的视觉路径。

Result: 在同一个冻结的2B基础模型上：音频pack将audio-to-text R@10相对同训练对照组提升+3.4至+5.4点，所有种子均为正，并在11倍数据量下复现；热成像pack在预注册验收门槛上约超额7倍（每个种子均达标），将thermal-to-text R@10从0.224提升至0.785。编码器替换实验定位到容量缺口——一个在CLAP式比较中优于Whisper系编码器的外部音频编码器，在冻结LLM内部反而落后16个R@10点，说明所需容量位于LLM各层中，正是门控适配器所放置的位置。已发布音频模型、热成像pack及训练/评估/不变性测试套件。

Conclusion: 通过按模态门控的深度适配器，可以在对既有输出零改动的前提下向多模态嵌入LLM可靠地添加新模态，其不改写文本路径的“不变性”性质经训练后仍成立并得到实证验证，且新增容量正确落在模型各层中，有效提升新模态的检索性能。

Abstract: Multimodal embedding models are deployed at scale: retrieval indices, benchmark results, and behavioral audits all depend on the base model's exact outputs. Extending such a model to a new modality with existing parameter-efficient methods silently changes those outputs; LoRA-style adaptation rewrites the text path whether or not the weights are merged, invalidating every stored embedding. We propose modality-gated deep adapters: bottleneck adapters attached to every decoder layer of a frozen multimodal embedding LLM, grouped into per-modality packs that execute only while their own modality is being encoded. The result is a modality added with zero change to existing outputs: inputs no pack claims traverse the base model's own computation graph, bit-for-bit unchanged, and co-loaded packs compose with an exact-zero isolation matrix. Both properties are stated as propositions, hold after arbitrary training rather than only at initialization, require no task labels or routing metadata at inference, and are verified by exact-equality tests on the released checkpoints. On one frozen 2B base, the audio pack (injected as connector tokens) improves audio-to-text R@10 by +3.4 to +5.4 points over an identically trained control, positive at every seed and reproduced at eleven times the data; the thermal pack, reusing the base's own frozen vision path, clears its pre-registered acceptance gate roughly sevenfold at every seed and lifts thermal-to-text R@10 from 0.224 to 0.785. An encoder swap locates the missing capacity: an external audio encoder that outranks Whisper-family encoders in CLAP-style comparisons loses by 16 R@10 points inside the frozen LLM, so the capacity belongs in the layers, exactly where the gated adapters place it. We release the audio model, the thermal pack, and the training, evaluation and invariance suites: models at huggingface.co/EximiusLabs, code on GitHub.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Static Charts: Can Language and Vision Language Models Generate Interactive Data Visualization Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26208)
*Mizanur Rahman,Aaryaman Kartha,Enamul Hoque Prince*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 VIS-GEN 基准，用于评估 LLM/VLM 从自然语言生成交互式数据可视化界面的能力，并设计多阶段生成框架以显著提升最佳模型通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据分析日益依赖语言驱动的交互式界面而非静态图表；现有 LLM/VLM 虽能生成静态图表，但生成交互式可视化界面的能力因缺乏基准而未被探索。

Method: 构建含 3,042 个样本的 VIS-GEN 基准，覆盖数据过滤、时序分析、可视化编辑等分析意图，并配备数据集元数据与逼真的目标驱动自然语言查询；同时提出结构化多阶段界面生成框架，将任务分解为可视化设计表示、多候选界面生成、约束感知批判与自我精炼。

Result: 在 14 个开源与闭源的先进 LLM/VLM 上评测，发现存在较大性能差距，且在隐式意图、多交互备选方案及复杂编辑操作上频繁失败；所提框架将最佳模型通过率提升 15.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: 交互式界面生成是超越静态图表合成的关键开放挑战；多阶段生成框架提供了一条通往更可靠语言驱动交互式可视化系统的可行路径，并已开源发布。

Abstract: Data visualization is central to analytical reasoning, but real-world analysis increasingly requires language-driven interactive interfaces rather than static charts. Although recent large language and vision language models (LLMs/VLMs) have shown promise in generating static charts from natural language, their ability to generate interactive data visualization interfaces remains largely unexplored due to the lack of benchmarks. We introduce VIS-GEN, a benchmark for evaluating how well LLMs/VLMs can generate interactive visualization interfaces from natural language queries. VIS-GEN comprises 3,042 samples covering diverse analytical intents, including data filtering, temporal analysis, and visualization editing, each paired with dataset metadata and natural language queries that are designed to reflect realistic, goal driven data exploration scenarios. We benchmark 14 state-of-the-art open-source and closed-source LLMs/VLMs, revealing large performance gaps and frequent failures on queries involving implicit intent, multiple interaction alternatives, and complex editing operations, highlighting interactive interface generation as a key open challenge beyond static chart synthesis. To address this, we propose a structured multi stage interface generation framework that decomposes the task into visualization design representation, generation of multiple interface candidates, constraint-aware critique, and self-refinement. This approach improves the best models pass rate by 15.9 percentage points, demonstrating a practical path toward more reliable language-driven interactive visualization systems. We release VIS-GEN at https://github.com/vis-nlp/VIS-GEN.

</details>


### [116] [Same Chart, Different Story: Bias in Vision-Language Chart Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26210)
*Mizanur Rahman,Huan Wu,Arash Asgari,Enamul Hoque Prince,Laleh Seyyed-Kalantari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChartBias，首个用于审计视觉语言模型（VLM）在图表解读中社会偏见的基准，发现三种普遍失效模式，并提出多智能体缓解框架显著减少叙事偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 越来越多地被用于解读图表并生成自然语言解释，但仅改变所提及的社会群体时可能产生不同叙事，从而强化刻板印象并误导决策。然而，此前缺乏系统评估跨社会维度图表解读偏见的基准。

Method: 构建 ChartBias 基准：包含 820 张人工整理的真实世界图表，覆盖种族、收入、年龄、宗教、移民身份和性别六种属性，生成 4,319 个有效（图表, 属性）实例和 8,638 对固定图表、仅替换群体术语的成对生成。在 12 个专有与开源 VLM 上共采集 155,484 条模型响应。提出多智能体缓解框架：将基于图表的证据提取与基于群体条件的生成分离，并用反事实裁判验证群体驱动的差异是否有图表证据支撑。

Result: 发现三种广泛存在的失效模式：叙事偏移（同一图表不同叙事）、群体幻觉（无证据地将图表归因于某群体）、偏好极性（有利趋势常与某一群体关联）。所提框架在保持图表实证推理的同时显著降低叙事偏移。

Conclusion: 评估图表理解不仅要衡量准确性，还要衡量跨社会群体的公平性与一致性。作者公开了 ChartBias 基准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to interpret charts and generate natural-language explanations for socially consequential data. However, they may produce different narratives for the same chart when only the referenced social group changes, reinforcing stereotypes and misleading decisions. Despite these risks, no benchmark exists for systematically evaluating bias in chart interpretation across social dimensions. We introduce ChartBias, the first benchmark for auditing bias in VLM-based chart interpretation. ChartBias contains 820 manually curated real-world charts spanning six attributes: race, income, age, religion, immigration status, and gender, yielding 4,319 valid chart, attribute instances and 8,638 paired generations where the chart is fixed and only the group term is swapped. Across 12 proprietary and open-source VLMs, totaling 155,484 model responses, we find three widespread failure modes: narrative shift (same chart, different narratives), group hallucination (assigning a chart to a group without evidence), and preference polarity (favourable trends often linked to one group). We further propose a multi-agent mitigation framework that serves as a strong baseline by separating chart-grounded evidence extraction from group-conditioned generation and using a counterfactual judge to verify that group-driven differences are supported by the chart. The framework substantially reduces narrative shift while preserving chart-grounded reasoning. Our findings show that evaluating chart understanding requires measuring not only accuracy, but also fairness and consistency across social groups. We release ChartBias at https://github.com/vis-nlp/ChartBiasBench.

</details>


### [117] [Damage Predicts Recovery: When Calibration Data Matters in Compressing Financial LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26241)
*Junyi Ye,Mengjia Yu,Debapriya Hazra,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 压缩（量化/剪枝）对下游任务造成的损失大小，而非校准语料与任务的领域匹配程度，决定了是否需要领域特定的校准数据。只有当压缩损伤较大时，任务格式化的领域数据才能帮助恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化和剪枝依赖小型校准语料，但金融等专业领域是否需要领域匹配的校准数据尚无定论。作者希望厘清"领域不匹配"与"压缩损伤"二者中哪个才是影响下游任务性能的关键因素。

Method: 作者提出假设：压缩是否保留目标能力决定校准语料的影响。在两种模型家族、六种压缩配置、三个词元匹配的校准语料和十个金融分类/数值问答任务上进行实验，对比量化与剪枝在下游任务上的损伤，以及通用语料与 FinMix（金融任务示例混合）等不同校准数据的恢复效果。

Result: 实验支持该假设：量化几乎不损失任务性能，此时更换校准语料影响很小；剪枝使数值问答准确率下降超过 40 个点，在这种受损场景下，通用语料无法帮助恢复，而 FinMix 能恢复大部分损失。损伤与恢复之间的关联在不同模型家族和规模下均成立。

Conclusion: 提出实用规则：先测量特定任务的压缩损伤，只有当损伤较大时才构建专门的校准数据。

Abstract: Post-training quantization and pruning rely on a small calibration corpus. Whether specialized domains such as finance require domain-matched calibration data remains unsettled. We argue that the answer depends on the task-level damage caused by compression rather than on domain mismatch. If compression preserves the target capability, changing the calibration corpus has little effect. If compression causes large losses, task-formatted calibration can recover part of the loss. We test this hypothesis across two model families, six compression configurations, three token-matched calibration corpora, and ten financial classification and numerical question-answering tasks. The results support this hypothesis. Quantization largely preserves task performance, and calibration choice has little effect in this case. Pruning reduces numerical QA accuracy by over 40 points. In these damaged settings, another generic corpus does not help, while FinMix, a mixture of financial task examples, recovers a large part of the loss. The link between damage and recovery holds across model families and scales. These findings support a practical rule. Measure task-specific compression damage first, and construct specialized calibration data only when the damage is large.

</details>


### [118] [PACE-dLLM: Elastic Block Decoding via Confidence Cliff Estimation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26249)
*Xiaocheng Lu,Shuhan Guo,Ziyue Ma,Jie Zhang,Jian Liu,Jingcai Guo,Haoxuan Che,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PACE-dLLM 是一种扩散语言模型加速方法，通过在每个步骤闭式拟合置信度"悬崖"曲线并将其饱和点作为前瞻窗口，同时独立设置置信度阈值决定令牌提交，从而解开前瞻水平与提交数量这两个耦合决策，在保证生成质量的同时显著提升解码吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLMs）已能在生成质量上与自回归 LLM 竞争，并支持原生并行解码，但其块式解码加速策略中的窗口长度 B 耦合了两个本应独立的决策：前瞻水平（看多远）和提交令牌数（提交多少）。现有加速器只能通过波动率跟踪、分隔符检测、学习打分等间接启发式来缓解这一耦合，缺乏对前瞻水平的直接判定机制。

Method: 作者提出 PACE-dLLM：观察到窗口内置信度通常呈上下文相关的"悬崖"形态，其饱和点直接标识合适的前瞻水平。方法在每个步骤以闭式形式拟合该参数化悬崖曲线，用其饱和点确定下一前瞻水平，并采用独立的置信度阈值进行令牌提交。基于"饱和收益"抽象，作者证明悬崖锚定的水平是达到最大有用单趟收益的最小水平。

Result: 在四个推理与代码基准上，PACE-dLLM 在两个开源 dLLM 骨干上均取得最佳平均准确率。相较于未加速的半自回归基线，平均端到端加速比 LLaDA 达 5.23 倍、Dream 达 3.06 倍（数学任务最高 8.52 倍），推进了质量-吞吐的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 基于置信度悬崖饱和点的前瞻水平设定是最优的：前瞻不足会恶化渐近 NFE 率，前瞻过度不会增加有用收益。PACE-dLLM 通过直接利用模型自身每步置信度中的结构化信息，以简单、闭式且可解释的方式实现优于间接启发式加速器的质量与速度权衡。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs), such as LLaDA and Dream, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) LLMs in generation quality while supporting native parallel decoding. A standard acceleration strategy is block-wise decoding, where each forward pass predicts a block of length B and commits high-confidence tokens. However, B couples two distinct decisions: the look-ahead horizon and the number of tokens to commit. Existing accelerators address this limitation through indirect heuristics, such as volatility tracking, delimiter detection, and learned scoring. In contrast, we show that the required information is already encoded in the model's own per-step confidence: in-window confidence typically follows a context-dependent cliff, whose saturation point directly identifies the appropriate look-ahead horizon. We propose PACE-dLLM, which fits this parametric cliff in closed form at each step, sets the next horizon by its saturation point, and uses an independent confidence threshold for token commitment. Under a saturated-yield abstraction, we show that the cliff-anchored horizon is the smallest horizon attaining maximal useful per-pass yield: fixed horizons that undershoot it incur a worse asymptotic NFE rate, while overshooting adds no useful yield. On four reasoning and code benchmarks, PACE-dLLM achieves the best average accuracy on both open-source dLLM backbones, with average wall-clock speedups of 5.23x on LLaDA and 3.06x on Dream (up to 8.52x on math) over the unaccelerated semi-AR baseline, advancing the quality-throughput Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [119] [CHiME-9 ECHI: A Machine Learning Challenge for Enhancing Conversations to Address Hearing Impairment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26306)
*Robert Sutherland,Thomas Kuebert,Marko Lugger,Stefan Petrausch,Eline Borch Petersen,Juan Azcarreta Ortiz,Buye Xu,Stefan Goetze,Jon Barker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CHiME-9挑战赛（针对听力受损人群的对话增强任务）及其结果：在嘈杂的自助餐厅环境中处理四人对话，从Meta Aria眼镜和助听器麦克风采集的多通道录音中提取对话伙伴的清晰语音，并通过主观与客观指标评估系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 听力受损人群在嘈杂环境中难以听清对话伙伴的语音，现有语音增强技术难以应对多说话人、干扰语音和复杂声效共存的真实场景。因此有必要设立标准化挑战任务，比较各系统在提升语音可懂度和质量方面的能力，推动面向助听场景的对话增强研究。

Method: 挑战模拟四人对话，环境为带有干扰语音源和音效的自助餐厅式噪声场景；使用Meta Aria眼镜与助听器麦克风采集多通道录音，并提供对话参与者的干净语音样本作为参考。任务是从含噪多通道录音中提取对话伙伴语音，采用客观指标与主观听力测试相结合的方式进行评估，并综合主观可懂度和质量对七个团队的提交系统进行排名。

Result: 共有七个团队提交系统。结果表明，客观指标与听众（主观）表现并不一致；尽管如此，排名靠前的系统在主观可懂度和质量评分上均相较挑战基线实现了显著提升。

Conclusion: 面向听力受损人群的CHiME-9对话增强挑战成功推动了该领域的研究；主观评测显示优秀系统能大幅超越基线，但客观指标与主观感知的脱节提示需开发更贴近人类听觉体验的评估方法。

Abstract: This work presents the task and results of the CHiME-9 challenge for Enhancing Conversations to address Hearing Impairment. The challenge considers the scenario of four-party conversations in a noisy, cafeteria-style environment with interfering speech sources and sound effects. Participants are provided with audio recordings made with Meta Aria glasses and hearing aid microphones, and clean speech samples of the conversation participants. The task is to extract the speech of the conversation partners from the noisy multi-channel recordings with the goal of improving the intelligibility and quality of the speech, evaluated using objective metrics and subjective listening tests. This paper reviews submissions from seven teams and ranks them on a combination of subjective intelligibility and quality. Results show that while the objective metrics do not reflect listener performance, the top systems were able to make substantial improvements over the challenge baseline in both intelligibility and quality ratings.

</details>


### [120] [Designing and Analysing Argument Mining Pipelines: Towards a Comprehensive Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26338)
*Siddharth Bhargava,Sara Tonelli,Patricia Martín-Rodilla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三视角框架（语言学、计算、领域），系统梳理端到端论辩挖掘管线的建模、计算与领域知识整合方式，并给出通用设计，为方法中心化的比较奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论辩挖掘（AM）将自然语言转化为论辩结构，通常依赖一系列任务构成的端到端管线；但不同方法对任务的划分与概念化方式各异，导致方法之间难以直接、透明地比较。因此需要一种更细粒度、面向任务层面的元分析，以实现清晰的方法对比与评估。

Method: 本文开展一项初步的元研究，系统综述若干最先进的端到端论辩挖掘工作，并通过“语言学视角—计算视角—领域视角”的三维框架分析其管线，考察这些管线如何将论辩建模为结构、如何进行计算，以及如何整合领域知识；并在此基础上提出对语言学与计算视角的通用化设计。

Result: 所提出的三视角框架揭示了不同端到端论辩挖掘方法在任务设计上的差异与共性，明确了关键论辩挖掘任务在结构建模与计算中的设计方式，并提供了一种通用设计作为参照。

Conclusion: 该框架为以方法论为中心描述各类论辩挖掘方法奠定了基础，有助于加深理解并支持未来更系统的比较研究。

Abstract: Argument Mining (AM) transforms natural language into its underlying argument structures. This transformation is typically realized through a sequence of AM tasks that form an end-to-end AM pipeline. However, AM approaches often differ in how they conceptualize these tasks, making direct comparisons between them difficult and opaque. This calls for a more nuanced, task-level analysis of AM approaches to enable clearer comparison and assessment.
  This work presents a preliminary meta-study that systematically reviews several state-of-the-art end-to-end AM works and analyzes their pipelines through a triple-perspective framework---a linguistic, computational and domain perspective---to understand how the pipelines model arguments as structures, computes them, and integrates domain knowledge. We further propose a general design to the linguistic and computational perspectives, illustrating how key AM tasks are designed for modeling and computation of argument structures. Our proposed framework lays the groundwork for methodology-centered descriptions across AM approaches, facilitating deeper understanding and more systematic comparisons in future research.

</details>


### [121] [Blaming Across the Aisle: Political Contrasting and Blame Attribution in the Danish Parliament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26346)
*Markus Lundsfryd Jensen,Rune Egeskov Trust,Kenneth Christian Enevoldsen,Sara Kolding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发了BlameBERT分类器，分析丹麦议会1997-2026年的指责归因话语，发现指责呈"香蕉形"轨迹（2016年前下降，2019-2026年显著持续上升），且政府地位、意识形态左右翼与意识形态极端性交互影响指责行为，指向政治话语的意识形态不对称硬化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治话语被认为愈发敌对，但缺乏稳健的实证证据；同时低中资源语言在指责归因任务上缺少高效标注的分类工具，制约了相关研究。

Method: 构建专门的BlameBERT分类器（F1: 0.80），采用面向低中资源语言的"标注高效"（annotation-efficient）流水线；将分类器应用于丹麦议会1997-2026年的语料，并结合多层级统计建模（multilevel statistical modeling）分析指责归因，辅以改变分类阈值的敏感性分析。

Result: 指责归因呈"香蕉形"轨迹：至2016年前下降，2019-2026年显著持续上升。政府地位持续影响指责（"政治对比"效应）：反对党指责显著多于执政党。该效应受意识形态调节：执政的指责抑制作用在右翼政党中较弱，且意识形态极端性在右翼更强地放大指责。近年来，左右翼与意识形态极端性的交互增强，显示右翼指责修辞出现意识形态硬化。敏感性分析表明结论对不同分类阈值稳健。

Conclusion: 政治语言敌对性的上升并非普遍性修辞漂移，而是集中在政治光谱右翼的意识形态不对称硬化，揭示了政治话语极化的结构性特征。

Abstract: Political discourse is widely perceived to be growing more hostile, yet robust evidence remains scarce. This study examines blame attribution in the Danish Parliament from 1997 to 2026, combining a purpose-built classifier, BlameBERT (F1: 0.80), with multilevel statistical modeling. The classifier is constructed using an annotation-efficient pipeline for blame attribution in low-to-mid resource languages. The results reveal a banana-shaped trajectory, with blame declining until around 2016 before entering a significant and sustained increase in recent years (2019-2026). Government status consistently influenced blame attribution - an effect we term political contrasting - with opposition parties blaming substantially more than governing parties. This effect was moderated by ideology: The blame-dampening effect of governing was less pronounced among right-wing parties, and ideological extremity amplified blame more strongly on the right. In recent years, the interaction between political wing and ideological extremity intensified, suggesting an ideological hardening of the blame rhetoric concentrated on the right of the political spectrum. Taken together, these patterns suggest that the perceived rise in harsh political language reflects not merely a general rhetorical drift, but an ideologically asymmetric hardening of political discourse. A sensitivity analysis showed that the conclusions were robust to varying classification thresholds.

</details>


### [122] [TransBERT: A Framework for Synthetic Translation in Domain-Specific Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26347)
*Julien Knafou,Luc Mottin,Anaïs Mottaz,Alexandre Flament,Patrick Ruch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TransBERT 框架与 TransCorpus 翻译工具包，通过纯合成翻译文本预训练语言模型，以解决非英语语言在专业领域数据稀缺的问题，并在法语生命科学领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业领域中非英语语言数据稀缺，严重限制了高效 NLP 工具的开发；需要一种可扩展的方法，在低资源语言/领域组合下构建高质量的 NLP 资源。

Method: 提出 TransBERT 预训练框架，仅使用合成翻译文本进行语言模型预训练；配套开发 TransCorpus 可扩展翻译工具包，用于生成大规模合成语料。以法语生命科学领域为实验对象，构建了 36.4GB 的法语生命科学语料 TransCorpus-bio-fr，并预训练得到 TransBERT-bio-fr 模型。

Result: 仅利用合成翻译数据，TransBERT-bio-fr 在多个下游任务上达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）性能，证明在高资源翻译方向上利用合成翻译构建低资源语言/领域 NLP 资源是可行的。

Conclusion: 合成翻译是构建高质量低资源语言/领域 NLP 资源的可行方案；作者公开了 TransCorpus 工具包、TransCorpus-bio-fr 语料、TransBERT-bio-fr 模型以及预训练与微调的可复现代码。

Abstract: The scarcity of non-English language data in specialized domains significantly limits the development of effective Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. We present TransBERT, a novel framework for pre-training language models using exclusively synthetically translated text, and introduce TransCorpus, a scalable translation toolkit. Focusing on the life sciences domain in French, our approach demonstrates that state-of-the-art performance on various downstream tasks can be achieved solely by leveraging synthetically translated data. We release the TransCorpus toolkit, the TransCorpus-bio-fr corpus (36.4GB of French life sciences text), TransBERT-bio-fr, its associated pre-trained language model and reproducible code for both pre-training and fine-tuning. Our results highlight the viability of synthetic translation in a high-resource translation direction for building high-quality NLP resources in low-resource language/domain pairs.

</details>


### [123] [HySparse2: Hybrid Sparse Attention with Two-Level KV Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26368)
*Jianyu Wei,Yizhao Gao,Qihao Zhang,Shimao Chen,Zhengju Tang,Yu Cheng,Shengjie Zhou,Zihan Jiang,Yifan Song,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Bo Yang,Gang Wang,Shijie Cao,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HySparse2 是一种两级 KV 共享的混合稀疏注意力架构，通过桥接自解码器与交叉解码器，使所有交叉解码器 KV 缓存均可由自解码器隐藏状态重建，从而让预填充在自解码器后提前退出，在长上下文检索与多轮智能体任务上以更低的预填充计算量和 KV 缓存开销超越 HySparse 和 Hybrid SWA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程、多轮智能体通常会生成短动作并处理来自工具和环境的冗长观测，导致上下文不断增长，这就要求高效的预填充、紧凑的 KV 缓存存储以及准确的长上下文检索能力。

Method: 提出 HySparse2，一种两级 KV 共享的混合稀疏注意力架构。外层采用 KV Bridging：借鉴 YOCO 的自解码器/交叉解码器结构，但只桥接全注意力层——自解码器使用混合滑动窗口注意力（SWA），交叉解码器使用混合稀疏注意力，且交叉解码器中全注意力层的 KV 缓存由自解码器全注意力层的隐藏状态生成。内层保留 HySparse 的 KV Reuse 设计并做两点改进：一是将块级稀疏替换为 token 级稀疏，以支持更细粒度的长上下文检索；二是移除稀疏层中独立的 SWA 分支，改为强制将最近 token 的滑动窗口纳入稀疏选择。

Result: 在 80B-A3B MoE 模型上，HySparse2 在长上下文检索和多轮智能体任务上均优于 HySparse 与 Hybrid SWA，同时大幅降低了预填充计算量和 KV 缓存存储。

Conclusion: 两级 KV 共享机制使所有交叉解码器 KV 缓存都能从自解码器隐藏状态构建，预填充可在自解码器之后退出并跳过全部交叉解码器层，从而在保持或提升性能的同时显著节省计算与存储资源。

Abstract: Long-horizon and multi-turn agents typically generate short actions and process long observations from tools and environments. This growing context demands efficient prefill, compact KV-cache storage, and accurate long-context retrieval. To meet these demands, we introduce HySparse2, a hybrid sparse attention architecture with two-level KV sharing. At the outer level, KV Bridging adopts a YOCO-style self-decoder and cross-decoder structure, but bridges only full-attention layers. The self-decoder uses hybrid sliding-window attention (SWA), while the cross-decoder uses hybrid sparse attention. The KV caches for full-attention layers in the cross-decoder are generated from the hidden states of full-attention layers in the self-decoder. At the inner level, HySparse2 retains HySparse's core KV Reuse design with two refinements. First, it replaces block-level sparsity with token-level sparsity for finer long-context retrieval. Second, it removes the separate SWA branch from sparse layers and instead forces a sliding window of recent tokens into the sparse selection. This two-level KV sharing allows all cross-decoder KV caches to be constructed from self-decoder hidden states. Prefill can therefore exit after the self-decoder, skipping all cross-decoder layers. On an 80B-A3B MoE model, HySparse2 outperforms HySparse and Hybrid SWA on long-context retrieval and multi-turn agentic tasks, while substantially reducing prefill computation and KV-cache storage.

</details>


### [124] [Layout-Guided Masking for GROBID: Lightweight Structural Gains in Large-Scale Scientific PDF Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26381)
*Luca Foppiano,Sana Khamassi,Vipul Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对学术PDF转机器可读全文的瓶颈，在CPU运行的GROBID解析器基础上，增加一个轻量级CPU检测器来定位图、表和页眉页脚页码等版式区域，通过类型化区域掩码将token路由到GROBID专用模型或丢弃，从而在无GPU、低成本条件下显著提升结构化提取精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模信息系统需要将学术PDF转为机器可读全文，现有基于视觉的解析器精度虽高但依赖GPU且可能引入噪声；而GROBID作为CPU上运行的模块化字体流解析器虽是事实标准，但缺乏对图、表及页眉页脚等版式区域的定位能力，导致表格检测和正文提取精度不足。本文旨在用轻量级CPU检测器增强GROBID，兼得CPU低成本与更高的结构化精度。

Method: 在GROBID字体流解析流程中集成一个轻量级CPU检测器，用于定位图形(figure)、表格(table)和副文本(paratext：页眉、页脚、页码)区域，将其编码为类型化区域掩码(typed-area masks)。据此将文本token路由到GROBID的专用模型，或对属于副文本的token直接丢弃。在Bioinformatics(1,926篇)和Materials Science(2,595篇)两个PMC语料上，以JATS为参照、使用节感知的结构化协议(section-aware structural protocol)进行评测；表格检测另用外部Table-BRGM基准，并以GriTS-Top衡量表格结构。

Result: 在两个PMC语料上，扩展方法在多数指标上优于原始GROBID（NS +0.025/+0.013；Materials Science段落召回+0.086，dz=1.08），且两个语料上的图题关联恢复均改善。外部Table-BRGM基准上，表格检测F1由0.16提升至0.94，表格结构GriTS-Top由0.27提升至0.78（仍低于最强GPU系统）。正文对比四个视觉系统（Docling、MinerU、olmOCR、dots.ocr），本方法在两个语料上有最佳段落精确率，在Materials Science上有最佳章节检测，字符错误率与最佳GPU解析器差0.004以内。端到端CPU成本比最便宜的GPU系统(Docling)低2.7–3.2倍，比生成式解析器低10–14倍。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级CPU区域检测器与类型化掩码路由增强GROBID，能在纯CPU、低成本条件下取得接近甚至部分超越GPU系统的结构化提取性能，尤其显著改善表格检测（F1 0.16→0.94）与表格结构恢复，并在段落精确率、章节检测和字符错误率上具备竞争力，为大规模文本语料构建提供了经济高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Transforming scholarly PDFs into machine-readable fulltext remains a bottleneck for large-scale information systems. Recent vision-based parsers improve accuracy, but need GPUs and may introduce noise into the extracted text. GROBID, a modular font-stream parser running on CPU, is the de-facto standard for structuring scientific articles and underpins several of the largest open scholarly corpora. We pair it with a lightweight CPU detector localising figure, table, and paratext (header, footer, page number) regions, encoded as typed-area masks whose tokens are routed to GROBID's specialised models or discarded. On two PMC corpora, Bioinformatics (1,926 articles) and Materials Science (2,595), scored against JATS with a section-aware structural protocol, our extension improves over plain GROBID on most metrics (NS $+0.025$/$+0.013$; $+0.086$ paragraph recall on Materials Science, $d_z{=}1.08$), and caption-linked figure recovery improves on both corpora. On the external Table-BRGM benchmark, table detection recovers F1 $0.16 \to 0.94$ and table structure follows (GriTS-Top $0.27 \to 0.78$, below the strongest GPU system). On body text, against four vision-based systems (Docling, MinerU, olmOCR, dots.ocr), it has the best paragraph precision on both corpora, the best section detection on Materials Science, and a character error rate within 0.004 of the best GPU parser. End-to-end on CPU, it costs $2.7$--$3.2\times$ less than the cheapest GPU system (Docling) and $10$--$14\times$ less than generative parsers.

</details>


### [125] [Combining Hierarchical Cognitive Process with Process Supervision for Interpretable Scene Safety Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26399)
*Zhiyun Jiang,Hanyong Wang,Binbin Liang,Yu Xie,Zhengjie Wang,Menglong Yang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将场景安全认知过程建模与过程监督相结合的方法：构建层次化认知安全结构并据此生成带过程标签的多步推理数据集，进而设计基于 LLM 的模块化过程监督框架（结合 LoRA 与 MoE），以提高场景安全理解的性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景安全理解在诸多关键领域关乎生死，但传统直接从场景到安全等级映射的方法缺乏可解释性，难以在关键应用中保证可靠性。为此，作者希望借鉴人类认知过程，赋予机器模型类似的安全推理认知能力，从而提升模型的可解释性与可信度。

Method: 首先构建层次化认知安全结构，据此开发一个基于多步推理且带过程标签的高质量场景安全理解数据集，既作基准也用于提升 LLM 的安全推理能力，并支持通过信息流与显著性分析对中间推理步骤做细粒度分析。在此基础上提出模块化、灵活的过程监督框架，以 LLM 为核心架构，引入 LoRA 与 MoE 策略，使各专家模块实现专门化与协作，分别负责整体推理链中的特定子过程。

Result: 系统实验评估表明，所提框架相比传统方法具有更优的可解释性和性能表现，验证了认知过程建模与过程监督相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将层次化认知结构与过程监督引入场景安全理解，能够显著提升模型的可解释性与安全推理性能，为关键领域的安全态势感知提供更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Scene safety understanding plays a life-or-death role in situational awareness in various critical domains. Traditional methods that rely on learning direct mappings between scenes and safety levels often lack interpretability, limiting their reliability in critical applications. An effective approach to overcoming this challenge lies in interpreting human cognitive processes and equipping machine models with analogous cognitive capabilities. This work explores an effective way of integrating scene safety cognitive process modeling and process supervision. Specifically, we first construct a hierarchical cognitive safety structure, which motivates the development of a novel, high-quality scene safety understanding dataset based on multi-step reasoning with process labels. This dataset serves both as a benchmark and a resource to improve the safety reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), while also enabling a granular analysis of intermediate reasoning steps through information flow and saliency-based techniques. Building upon this foundation, we introduce a modular and flexible process supervision framework that reflects the hierarchical nature of human cognition. This framework leverages LLMs as the core architecture and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation(LoRA) and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) strategies to enable specialization and collaboration among expert modules, each tasked with specific sub-processes of the overall reasoning chain. Systematic experimental evaluations and analyses confirm that our framework exhibits superior interpretability and performance characteristics compared to traditional approaches.

</details>


### [126] [Enriching Speech Emotion Representations with Conversational Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26422)
*Arthur Peuvot,Romaric Besançon,Gaël de Chalendar,Bianca Vieru,Ioana Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ACERT 模块，通过引入可变长度的对话上下文窗口来捕捉口语交互中的情感演变，在多个数据集上超越现有 SOTA，并验证其收益来源于情感与对话的连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感识别（SER）大多在话语（utterance）级别预测情感，忽略了对话上下文、情感流动以及说话人之间的交互，限制了系统准确、自适应地与人类交互的能力。

Method: 提出 ACERT（Averaged Contextual Emotion Representation through Time）模块，将可变长度（灵活窗口）的对话上下文整合进来，以更好地捕捉口语交互中情感的时序演变。

Result: ACERT 在 IEMOCAP 上超越当前 SOTA，在 SAFE 上建立了首个上下文感知基准，并在 MELD 上以未加权、类别均衡指标取得优异结果。消融实验显示其增益来自情感与对话的连续性，而非说话人身份或声学条件。

Conclusion: ACERT 通过建模对话上下文中的情感连续性，显著提升了语音情感识别的鲁棒性与准确性，证明了上下文信息对 SER 的重要价值。

Abstract: Detecting emotions is necessary for building systems that can accurately and adaptively interact with humans. Speech Emotion Recognition (SER) has become an important research focus to develop intelligent spoken interfaces. However, most studies predict emotions at the utterance level, ignoring the conversational context, along with the emotional flow and speaker interactions it carries. In this paper, we introduce ACERT (Averaged Contextual Emotion Representation through Time), a module that integrates a flexible-length window of conversational context to better capture emotional evolution in spoken interactions. To evaluate the robustness of this method, we conducted experiments on datasets spanning diverse emotionally expressive styles and contexts. ACERT outperforms current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches on IEMOCAP, establishes the first context-aware benchmark on SAFE, and obtains strong results on MELD for unweighted, class-balanced metrics. Ablation studies show that ACERT's gains come from emotional and conversational continuity, rather than from speaker identity or acoustic conditions.

</details>


### [127] [How to Estimate Whether You Have Found Several Needles in a Haystack: Measuring Calibration in Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26468)
*Sophie Henning,Georg Hofmann,Alexander Schulte,Alexander Fraser,Annemarie Friedrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多标签分类中的置信度校准常被忽视，现有label-wise binning方法会低估误差或受标签频率主导。本文提出给正负标签分配等权的新binning方案，得到更有意义的多标签校准误差度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签任务（如医疗编码、新闻主题）常被大量负标签主导，而现有校准指标多针对二分类或多分类。现有label-wise ECE的binning方案要么低估误差、要么仅反映标签频率，或产生许多样本极少的bin，无法提供可信的校准误差估计。

Method: 提出一种新的binning方案，对正标签和负标签分配给予相等权重，用于计算label-wise expected calibration error（ECE），从而在多标签设置下得到可信的校准误差度量。

Result: 实证研究表明，与现有binning方案相比，新方案在层次多标签和极端多标签分类中能给出有意义的校准误差估计；同时发现大语言模型在多标签预测上的置信度校准仍是一个开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作为多标签分类提供了一个可靠的校准评估指标，并揭示了LLM多标签校准的开放难题，为进一步研究奠定基础。

Abstract: A key factor in deciding whether to trust an automatic prediction is its confidence score, which should be calibrated to match the actual probability of the prediction being correct. Most confidence calibration metrics target binary or multi-class tasks, while multi-label calibration remains largely underexplored. Multi-label classification tasks, such as assigning medical codes to clinical notes or determining news topics, are usually dominated by a large number of negatives, i.e., labels that do not apply. We show that existing binning schemes to compute label-wise expected calibration error either underestimate the error, simply reflect label frequency, or suffer from many bins with very few instances. To achieve trustworthy label-wise calibration errors, we propose a new binning scheme that gives equal weight to positive and negative label assignments. Our empirical study demonstrates that in contrast to existing binning schemes, our new scheme results in meaningful estimates of calibration error in hierarchical and in extreme multi-label classification. We also show that calibrating confidence scores of large language models for multi-label predictions is an open challenge. Our detailed analysis lays the foundation for further research by providing a solid evaluation metric for measuring calibration in multi-label classification.

</details>


### [128] [Spoken Language Models that Think Aloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26488)
*Junyi Ao,Kainan Peng,Mingbo Ma,Shun Zhang,Zhenyu Tang,Xutai Ma,Xiang Li,Yinghao Li,Yuancheng Wang,Zhizheng Wu,Haizhou Li,Qing He,Xubo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对语音语言模型在串行"先想后说"范式下推理时产生过长静音、破坏实时口语交互的问题，本文提出一种基于 Thinker-Talker 架构的异步有声思考（think-aloud）框架，通过主推理流与轻量有声思考流配合及动态平衡策略，在保持准确率的前提下大幅减少用户可感知的静音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思考（CoT）虽然提升了语言模型的推理能力，但直接用于语音语言模型（SLMs）时，串行的"先想后说"模式会产生长时间静默，破坏实时口语交互体验。因此需要在不牺牲推理质量的前提下，缩短推理阶段用户可感知的静音。

Method: 提出异步有声思考框架，构建于 Thinker-Talker 架构之上。框架包含两条并行的流：主推理流负责逻辑推导，轻量有声思考流根据用户输入和不断演进的推理状态生成简短、与任务相关的过程性语音。运行时通过动态平衡策略协调两条流，在静音即将出现时触发额外的有声思考语音，并在最终回答就绪时取消待播报语句。

Result: 在口语推理和问答基准上的实验表明，该方法显著减少了推理过程中用户可感知的静音，同时回答准确率与串行"先想后说"基线相当。

Conclusion: 异步有声思考框架展示了在语音语言模型中实现响应式交互的潜力，能够在维持推理性能的同时有效消除静音空档，改善实时口语交互体验。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has improved the capability of language models, directly applying it to Spoken Language Models (SLMs) may introduce long silent intervals under the serial "think-then-speak" paradigm, disrupting real-time spoken interaction. To address this issue, we propose an asynchronous think-aloud framework for reasoning-based SLMs within the Thinker-Talker architecture. The framework maintains a primary reasoning stream for logical deduction and a lightweight think-aloud stream that generates short, task-grounded progress utterances conditioned on the user input and the evolving reasoning state. A dynamic balance strategy coordinates the two streams at runtime, triggering additional think-aloud speech to avoid silent gaps and canceling pending utterances when the final response becomes ready. Experiments on spoken reasoning and question-answering benchmarks show that our approach substantially reduces user-audible silence during reasoning while maintaining answer accuracy comparable to that of a serial "think-then-speak" baseline, demonstrating the potential of asynchronous think-aloud for responsive interaction in SLMs.

</details>


### [129] [Calibration as a First-Class Criterion in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26489)
*Mario Sanz-Guerrero,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出语言模型校准虽已有成熟度量方法，但未在NLP研究主流中被广泛采用，这一采纳差距是可信LLM评估的主要障碍；作者呼吁各子领域将校准分数与其主要性能指标并列报告，并把校准视为每个模型的必备属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准（模型表达的或隐含的置信度与经验正确性之间的一致性）已有现成度量方法，但在校准子领域之外，NLP研究引入新模型、数据集和基准时普遍不检查置信度分数是否有意义。这种"采纳差距"导致两方面问题：部署时过度自信的错误会造成实际伤害；研究流程内部，LLM-as-a-judge、合成数据生成、主动学习等方法依赖校准置信度却未加以验证。作者视这一采纳差距为可信LLM评估的主要障碍。

Method: 本文为观点/立场性论文，不提出新的算法。其论证路径是：指出校准度量的低门槛要求——标准校准指标每个样本仅需两个输入（置信度分数与正确性判断），而现有大多数基准已同时提供这两者，因此校准可立即报告；同时承认开放式生成中如何定义这两个输入仍是开放挑战。据此提出倡议：每个NLP子领域应将其主要性能指标与一个校准分数配套报告。

Result: 论文的核心"结果"是论证了一个现状与呼吁：校准度量已具备且成本极低（每例仅需置信度与正确性两个输入，现有基准多已具备），但采纳率远未达标；开放式生成场景下置信度与正确性的定义仍是未解决的开放问题，阻碍校准在此类任务中的普及。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁将校准从子领域内的冷门话题提升为每个模型的必备属性，要求各NLP子领域在报告主要性能指标的同时附带校准分数，以弥合采纳差距，从而改善部署安全与研究流程（如LLM-as-a-judge、合成数据、主动学习）的可信度。

Abstract: Calibration of language models -- the alignment between expressed or implicit confidence and empirical correctness -- is a well-studied subfield within NLP. Methods to measure it already exist. The problem is adoption: outside this subfield, NLP research regularly introduces new models, datasets, and benchmarks without checking whether the model's confidence scores are meaningful. We argue that this adoption gap is a major obstacle to trustworthy LLM evaluation. Miscalibration causes problems in two distinct areas: at deployment, where overconfident mistakes cause real harm, and inside the research pipeline, where methods like LLM-as-a-judge, synthetic data generation, and active learning rely on calibrated confidence without verifying it. Standard calibration metrics only require two inputs per example: a confidence score and a correctness judgment. Most benchmarks in use today already provide both, meaning calibration can be reported immediately. For open-ended generation, however, defining these two inputs is still an open challenge. We argue that each NLP subfield should pair its main performance metric with a calibration score and call for treating calibration as an essential property of every model rather than a niche topic.

</details>


### [130] [A Semiotics-Aware Framework for Evaluating Fidelity and Coverage in Natural Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26527)
*Lorenzo Zangari,Davide Picca*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种评估文本间"符号学对齐"的框架，通过符号学保真度与符号学覆盖度两个分数，衡量两段文本在语境意义与话语指涉上的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两段文本描述同一表达时，基于词法重叠或整文本相似度的标准指标可能无法捕捉其框架方式上的有意义差异，因此需要一个能刻画文本如何构建意义与凸显话语指涉的评估方法。

Method: 构建符号学画像（涵盖文本的语境意义和凸显的话语指涉），并据此计算两个分数：符号学保真度（一段文本的画像在多大程度上被另一段支持）与符号学覆盖度（一段文本在多大程度上还原了另一段的画像）。

Result: 实验表明覆盖度通常低于保真度；此外，LLM 与人工整理数据之间的对齐在低采样温度下最高，而提高温度会降低这种对齐。

Conclusion: 该框架能够捕捉传统指标无法识别的框架差异，并揭示采样温度对 LLM 与人类数据符号学对齐的影响，为文本语义对齐评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: When two texts describe the same expression, standard metrics based on lexical overlap or whole-text similarity may fail to detect meaningful differences in how that expression is framed. We propose a framework to evaluate semiotic alignment between texts, where a semiotic profile encompasses both the contextual meaning and the discourse references made salient by a text. Our approach yields two scores, Semiotic Fidelity and Semiotic Coverage, estimating how much of one text's profile is supported by the other and how much of the other's profile it recovers. Experiments show that coverage is typically lower than fidelity, and that alignment between LLMs and human-curated data is highest at low sampling temperatures, while higher temperatures reduce this alignment.

</details>


### [131] [Transcribe, Translate, and Optimize: Joint Reward Learning for Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26536)
*Yanghe Dong,Wanting Huang,Weiran Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用群体相对策略优化（GRPO）对 LLM 语音翻译进行联合识别与翻译微调，以解决基于转录的思维链（CoT）方法在训练-推理间存在的转录不匹配问题。实验表明 CoT GRPO 在 CoVoST 2 和 FLEURS 上均优于 Direct ST GRPO 和 CoT SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于 LLM 的语音翻译中，transcription-based 思维链（CoT）在监督微调（SFT）阶段使用的是参考转录，而在推理阶段使用的是模型自身生成的转录，二者存在训练-推理不匹配（training-inference mismatch），这会损害翻译质量。

Method: 提出通过群体相对策略优化（GRPO）进行联合识别与翻译微调：对转录和翻译都进行评分，且翻译以模型自身生成的转录为条件（而非参考转录）；同时比较了三种 token 优势（token advantage）策略。模型使用 Qwen2.5-Omni-3B，覆盖四种语言，在 CoVoST 2 上训练，并在 CoVoST 2 和 FLEURS 上测试，对比了 CoT 与直接语音翻译（Direct ST）在 SFT 和 GRPO 下的表现。

Result: CoT GRPO 在 CoVoST 2 和 FLEURS 上分别比 Direct ST GRPO 高出 1.77 和 0.83 个平均 BLEU 点。相比 CoT SFT，GRPO 将 BLEU 提升 0.82 和 0.67 点，并相对降低词错误率（WER）8.8% 和 7.2%。

Conclusion: 强化微调（GRPO）能够有效缓解语音翻译中训练-推理的转录不匹配问题，同时提升识别与翻译质量，表明该方法是改进基于 LLM 的 CoT 语音翻译的有效途径。

Abstract: In LLM-based speech translation, transcription-based chain-of-thought (CoT) suffers from a mismatch between reference transcripts used in supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and model-generated transcripts at inference. To address this, we propose joint recognition and translation fine-tuning via group relative policy optimization (GRPO). We score both transcripts and translations, with translation conditioned on model-generated transcripts, and compare three token advantage strategies. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-3B across four languages, we evaluate CoT against direct speech translation (Direct ST) under SFT and GRPO, training on CoVoST 2 and testing on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. CoT GRPO outperforms Direct ST GRPO by 1.77 and 0.83 average BLEU points on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. Compared to CoT SFT, GRPO boosts BLEU by 0.82 and 0.67 points and reduces word error rate (WER) by 8.8% and 7.2% relatively. These results highlight reinforcement fine-tuning as an effective method to mitigate the training-inference mismatch, jointly improving recognition and translation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过Upworthy真实A/B测试数据验证LLM作为"合成人设"预测受众点击行为的有效性，发现无角色扮演的零样本基线显著优于人设面板，表明合成人设在预测聚合参与度上不仅无效反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销人员日益依赖LLM作为"合成人设"来预测受众反应，且已有证据显示以画像条件化的LLM能模仿人类样本。但这一预测在真实行为上是否成立、人设机制是否真的有用，尚缺乏对照真实数据的验证，故需开展sim-to-real有效性研究。

Method: 以Upworthy研究档案（数千个共享真实流量的标题A/B测试及实测点击率）作为留出的真实基准。对比两种方法：一是基于真实受众人口统计的十人设面板；二是无角色扮演的零样本基线（直接询问模型典型读者点击的可能性）。在399个统计上可区分的可靠子集上测量有效性，并在三个独立Upworthy切分、异域新闻数据集、不同随机种子/提示措辞/模型（三个Gemini层级及OpenAI gpt-4.1）上做稳健性与复现检验。

Result: 其一，真实基准的可靠性是约束瓶颈——多数A/B测试无统计显著胜者，有效性只能在可靠子集（n=399）上度量。其二，人设条件化降低了预测有效性：无角色基线排序表现明显更好（Kendall τ=0.361，中等效应；top-1准确率49.2%），而人设面板τ=0.084、top-1准确率34.6%，置信区间不重叠。直接询问模型可汲取准确的人群先验，强制角色扮演则注入偏差与噪声。结果在三个Upworthy切分上复现，在异域新闻数据上方向一致，且对种子、提示措辞、模型选择稳健（含与gpt-4.1的显著配对差异）。

Conclusion: 对预测聚合参与度而言，朴素的LLM排序器优于人设模拟——合成人设不仅是弱预测器，而且比不使用它们更差。研究提供公开的、artifact-first的复现包。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [133] [VideoX-Qwen: Data-Centric Instruction-Based Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26015)
*JJiahang Li,Dingbao Shao,Xinyu Chen,Song Wu,Jiang Lin,Duo Li,Yuhang Liu,Jiaxin Hu,Shengrong Gu,Ying Tai,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VideoX-Qwen 提出了一套大规模数据生产管线与统一模型训练框架，用于通用指令驱动的视频编辑；管线产出超 120 万条定向编辑数据，并训练出基于 Qwen-Wan 的统一编辑模型，在多项指标上超过 UniVideo 和 Kling O1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视频编辑的进展受限于两个瓶颈：(1) 缺少大规模成对的（源视频-指令-目标视频）监督数据；(2) 现有视频生成骨干难以有效适配为指令驱动的编辑模型。与生成不同，编辑必须在执行指定变换的同时保留无关主体、场景结构、运动与时序连续性，因此需要同时解决数据与训练框架两方面的问题。

Method: 作者提出 VideoX-Qwen 框架，包含两条主线：(1) 数据构建：一个可扩展的生产管线将多种专用生成模型与理解模型组织成互补路径，分别覆盖添加、移除、替换和属性编辑四类任务，后进行质量筛选与指令增广，产出跨任务的结构化源-指令-目标三元组数据；(2) 模型训练：开发统一的 Qwen-Wan 编辑器，融合多模态语义条件与稠密源视频潜在引导，并采用“图像-视频”渐进式训练策略，依次对齐多模态指令接口、将视频生成器适配为源条件编辑、并用精选高分辨率数据优化输出质量。

Result: 生产管线产出超过 120 万条定向视频编辑记录，其中每个主要任务组均超过 40 万条，自动接受率为 89%。在 100 个样例的对比中，VideoX-Qwen 在 11 项报告指标中的 9 项上取得最佳平均成绩，涵盖指令遵循、编辑质量、内容保留、结构与感知相似性以及视频分布质量，优于 UniVideo 和 Kling O1。

Conclusion: VideoX-Qwen 的大规模数据生产系统与统一训练框架为更强的指令驱动视频编辑奠定了实用基础，表明通过结构化配对监督与源条件训练的协同，可以显著提升通用视频编辑能力。

Abstract: Progress in general-purpose video editing depends on constructing large-scale paired supervision and effectively adapting video-generation backbones to instruction-driven editing. Unlike video generation, video editing must execute a requested transformation while preserving unrelated subjects, scene structure, motion, and temporal continuity. We present VideoX-Qwen, an integrated data-construction and model-training framework for general instruction-based video editing. Our scalable production pipeline organizes specialized generation and understanding models into complementary routes for addition, removal, replacement, and attribute editing, followed by quality screening and instruction enrichment. It produces more than 1.2 million directional video-editing records, including over 400,000 records in each major task group, with an automatic acceptance rate of 89%. The resulting corpus provides broad and structured coverage of common editing operations through a unified source-instruction-target interface. We further develop a unified Qwen-Wan editor that combines multimodal semantic conditioning with dense source-video latent guidance. A progressive image-video training strategy aligns the multimodal instruction interface, adapts the video generator to source-conditioned editing, and refines output quality with selected high-resolution data.
